### [1] [Floquet Instability of Photon-Axion Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26908)
*Yuuki Sugiyama,Kazushige Ueda*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在时空周期性背景磁场中轴子-光子转换的动力学行为，利用洛伦兹变换将其映射为时间周期系统，并应用Floquet理论揭示了一种由Floquet副本共振交叉引发的不稳定性；该不稳定性导致复Floquet指数和指数增长模，显著增强由初始光子场激发的轴子场振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子的发现将提供超出标准模型的新物理证据；而传统静态或仅空间/时间周期磁场下的轴子-光子转换机制已较成熟，亟需探索更一般时空依赖背景下的新动力学效应。

Method: 采用洛伦兹变换将含时空周期磁场的耦合轴子-光子系统转化为时间周期线性系统，继而应用Floquet定理分析其本征谱结构，识别不稳定模式与复Floquet指数，并通过微扰方法确定不稳定性发生的参数区域。

Result: 发现Floquet不稳定性——源于不同Floquet副本间的共振交叉，产生复Floquet指数及指数增长解；该不稳定性可在特定参数区间内显著放大由入射光子激发的轴子场振幅。

Conclusion: 时空周期性磁场构成一种新型轴子-光子转换调控机制，其核心是Floquet不稳定性，为轴子探测提供了新的理论路径与实验设计思路。

Abstract: The discovery of axions would provide evidence for physics beyond the Standard Model. In this work, we investigate axion-photon conversion in the presence of a background magnetic field with simultaneous spatial and temporal periodicity. By performing a Lorentz transformation, the coupled axion-photon system is mapped onto a time-periodic linear system that can be analyzed using Floquet theorem. We find unstable modes associated with resonant crossing between Floquet replicas. The resulting complex Floquet exponents lead to exponentially growing modes, which we identify as a Floquet instability. Then, we determine the parameter regions in which this instability appears in a perturbative analysis. We further show that the Floquet instability enhances the axion-field amplitude generated from an initial photon field. Our results demonstrate that spatiotemporally periodic magnetic backgrounds provide a distinct mechanism for modifying axion-photon conversion through Floquet instability.

</details>


### [2] [Nonlocality effects on fermions confined within a hard-wall potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27349)
*G. Q. Garcia,P.J. Porfírio,E. Brito,D. C. Moreira*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了非局域性效应对自旋-1/2费米子在硬壁势约束下的能谱的影响，基于一个具有修正动能项的非局域狄拉克理论，在柱坐标下推导场方程；通过平方狄拉克算符的本征谱将无限阶方程约化为有限阶问题，并证明在与局域狄拉克理论连续相连的分支上，非局域修正可等效为有效费米子质量；进而解析求解了圆柱形硬壁和量子环（双同心柱面边界）两种几何构型下的能谱，径向方程归结为贝塞尔方程，边界条件导致模式量子化；局域极限Λ→∞恢复标准结果，而有限非局域性引起有效质量偏移，从而改变粒子–反粒子能量分裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非局域物理效应对量子受限费米系统能谱的可观测影响，尤其关注其如何修改粒子–反粒子对称性和质量标度行为。

Method: 从非局域狄拉克理论出发，采用柱坐标系构建场方程；利用平方狄拉克算符的本征值谱实现微分方程阶数截断；结合硬壁边界条件求解径向薛定谔类方程，归结为贝塞尔函数本征值问题。

Result: 获得圆柱硬壁与量子环两种几何下解析能谱；证明非局域性等效于引入动量依赖的有效质量m_eff(p)，且在低能连续分支上可近似为常数修正；能级间距与粒子–反粒子能量分离随非局域尺度Λ单调变化。

Conclusion: 非局域性在量子约束费米系统中产生可计算、可观测的谱学效应，其核心表现是有效质量重整化，为检验紫外完备理论（如弦理论或非局域QFT）提供了潜在低能探针。

Abstract: We investigate nonlocality effects on the energy spectrum of spin-$1/2$ fermions confined by hard-wall potentials. Starting from a nonlocal Dirac theory with a modified kinetic term, we formulate the associated field equation in flat spacetime using cylindrical coordinates. By exploiting the eigenvalue spectrum of the squared Dirac operator, the original infinite-order equation is reduced to a finite-order problem. We also show that on the branch continuously connected to the local Dirac theory the nonlocal corrections can be encoded in an effective fermion mass. We then find analytical energy spectra for two confining geometries: a cylindrical hard wall and a quantum ring defined by two concentric cylindrical boundaries. In both cases, the radial equations reduce to Bessel equations and the boundary conditions quantize the allowed modes. The local hard-wall spectra are recovered for $\Lambda\to\infty$, whereas finite nonlocality shifts the mass contribution and, consequently, the particle--antiparticle energy separation. These results provide a direct characterization of nonlocal effects in quantum-confined fermionic systems.

</details>


### [3] [Probing Lepton Flavor Violation at FCC-ee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27057)
*L. Allwicher,R. Bartocci,M. Cepeda,C. Cornella,L. P. S. Leal,A. Lusiani,C. Martin Perez,P. Paradisi,E. Scantamburlo,O. Sumensari*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在FCC-ee Z极点运行下，利用约2×10¹¹个τ轻子对，在SMEFT框架中探测轻子味破坏（LFV）的潜力；τ衰变（如τ→3ℓ、τ→ℓγ）在多数情形下比Z→τℓ探测量子更灵敏，并覆盖更广的SMEFT算符空间；而h→τℓ对Yukawa型LFV最强，但对辐射诱导的半轻子型LFV，τ衰变反成主导探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LFV研究多聚焦于Z→τℓ和h→τℓ过程，但τ轻子衰变在高统计量FCC-ee下可能提供更强且互补的新物理探针能力，尤其在SMEFT系统性分析中尚未被充分评估。

Method: 在SMEFT框架下，将τ→3ℓ、τ→ℓγ、Z→τℓ及h→τℓ等LFV过程统一映射至维度6算符；结合FCC-ee Z极点预期积分亮度与本底估计，计算各过程对不同算符系数的灵敏度极限，并进行跨过程比较。

Result: τ衰变对多数四费米子及光子耦合类SMEFT算符的灵敏度超越Z→τℓ（提升因子30–800），并覆盖Z→τℓ无法敏感的算符（如C_{eφ}、C_{φℓ}^{(3)}）；h→τℓ仅在Yukawa型算符（如C_{eφ}）中占优；而对辐射诱导h→τℓ的半轻子算符（如C_{ℓq}^{(1,3)}），τ衰变反成最灵敏探针。

Conclusion: FCC-ee的τ工厂模式是SMEFT中LFV研究不可替代的核心平台：τ衰变与h→τℓ构成正交互补探针，联合分析可完整约束LFV SMEFT参数空间，尤其对区分新物理紫外起源具有关键鉴别力。

Abstract: We investigate the prospects for probing Lepton Flavor Violation (LFV) at FCC-ee within the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) framework. The approximately $2 \times 10^{11}$ $\tau$-lepton pairs expected at the $Z$-pole run of FCC-ee could improve current limits on the branching fractions of $\tau\to3\ell$ and $\tau\to\ell\gamma$ ($\ell=e,\mu$) by factors ranging from 30 to 800, depending on the specific channel. We compare the resulting new-physics reach with that obtained from searches for $Z \to \tau \ell$ and $h \to \tau \ell$, which have so far received greater attention in studies of LFV at future $e^+e^-$ colliders. We find that $\tau$ decays can surpass both the current and the projected sensitivity of $Z\to\tau\ell$ searches to the same LFV interactions, while probing a broader range of SMEFT operators. We further show that, for LFV Yukawa-like interactions, direct searches for $h\to\tau\ell$ provide the strongest sensitivity. By contrast, for semileptonic operators that radiatively induce LFV Higgs decays, this hierarchy can be reversed, with $\tau$ decays providing the leading probe.

</details>


### [4] [Two-loop helicity amplitudes for $gg\to W^+W^-$ using high-energy and forward expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27092)
*Mateusz Czaja,Ben Rott,Matthias Steinhauser,Daniel Stremmer*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了双胶子散射产生两个离壳W玻色子（gg → W⁺W⁻）过程的QCD次领头阶修正，特别考虑了圈图中顶夸克的虚贡献；采用多区域相空间展开方法：高能极限（s,t ≫ mₜ）、前向散射极限（t → 0）及大顶夸克质量极限（mₜ ≫ √s），并验证了所有螺旋度振幅在整个相空间的数值精度与参数显式依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确预言强子对撞机上WW产生截面需包含顶夸克质量效应的NLO QCD修正，尤其在W玻色子离壳、高能或前向区域，固定阶微扰计算面临红外/紫外发散与数值不稳定问题。

Method: 采用多尺度渐近展开技术：1) 高能展开（s, t ≫ mₜ），保留mₜ作为小参数；2) 前向极限展开（t → 0），解析处理t-道奇点结构；3) 大mₜ展开（mₜ ≫ √s），作为一致性检验；所有展开统一于完整圈积分约化与张量积分解析表达式。

Result: 获得全部螺旋度振幅的解析/半解析NLO QCD修正表达式，在全相空间达到亚百分比级数值精度；明确给出对αₛ、mₜ、M_W、Γ_W等输入参数的显式依赖关系；三类展开相互交叉验证，证实无遗漏物理效应。

Conclusion: 该工作为LHC上离壳WW产生过程提供了首个具备全参数依赖性与高精度相空间覆盖的NLO QCD理论预言，解决了顶夸克质量效应在高能/前向运动学下的系统处理难题，为BSM信号提取与标准模型检验奠定坚实基准。

Abstract: We calculate next-to-leading order QCD corrections for the process $gg \to W^+W^-$ with two off-shell $W$ bosons, accounting for virtual top quarks in the loop diagrams. Our approach relies on combining expansions valid in different regions of phase space. Specifically, we perform a high-energy expansion in which the Mandelstam variables are much larger than the top-quark mass. Furthermore, we expand around the forward-scattering limit, where the Mandelstam variable $t$ is small. We also provide an expansion for large top-quark mass, which serves as an important cross-check. We provide technical details regarding the various expansions and demonstrate that precise results are obtained for all helicity amplitudes across the entire phase space. Our results exhibit an explicit dependence on all input parameters.

</details>


### [5] [This is SParTA: A modular, efficient and scalable transformer architecture for jet-tagging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27106)
*Rajneil Baruah,Subhadeep Mondal,Sunando Kumar Patra,Satyajit Roy*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: SParTA是一种面向高能对撞机喷注标记任务的稀疏粒子相互作用Transformer架构，通过将粒子按p_T排序并分层处理（硬核二次交互+软成分线性交叉注意力），在显著降低参数量与计算开销的同时，达到或超越现有主流模型（如ParT、IAFormer、ParticleNet）的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准Transformer在喷注建模中因全连接自注意力导致的O(l²)内存与计算瓶颈，尤其在软辐射成分数量大时；同时保留物理相关的成对相互作用与色流信息。

Method: 提出两阶段拓扑处理：(1) 对p_T最高的N个硬组分执行基于四动量与全局喷注子结构观测量动态生成的成对物理/学习关系特征的O(N²)微分交互注意力；(2) 其余l−N个软组分通过O(lN)交叉注意力聚合至硬核表征，避免O(l²)复杂度。

Result: 在夸克-胶子区分任务中，300K参数SParTA达AUC=92.02%、准确率85.06%，性能匹敌2.14M参数ParT；在30%信号效率下背景压低能力比IAFormer优21%；在强子化顶夸克标记任务中，511K参数模型超越ParT与ParticleNet。

Conclusion: SParTA验证了在粒子物理喷注表征学习中，结合物理先验（p_T排序、四动量关系、子结构观测量）与稀疏注意力机制可实现高效、可扩展且高性能的建模范式。

Abstract: We present the Sparse Particle-interaction Transformer Architecture (SParTA), a modular, scalable, and efficient transformer for jet tagging at high-energy colliders. Exploiting the steeply falling transverse-momentum ($p_T$) spectrum of jet constituents, SParTA factorizes constituent processing into two topological regimes. Ordered by descending $p_T$, only the $N$ hardest constituents (the hard core) participate in quadratic $O(N^2)$ differential interaction attention driven by pairwise physical and learned relational features. The remaining soft constituents are integrated via linear $O(lN)$ cross-attention to the hard core, preserving soft-radiation and color-flow information while avoiding the $O(l^2)$ memory bottleneck of full self-attention over all $l$ constituents. Pairwise interaction features are computed dynamically in-network from constituent four-momenta and conditioned on global jet-level substructure observables. On the quark-gluon tagging benchmark, a 300K-parameter SParTA model achieves an AUC of 92.02% and accuracy of 85.06%, matching the 2.14M-parameter Particle Transformer (ParT) at one-seventh the parameter count and outperforming IAFormer by 21% in background rejection at 30% signal efficiency. On the boosted top-tagging benchmark, SParTA outperforms ParT and ParticleNet with 511K parameters, while we analyze the architectural trade-offs relative to fully dense multi-layer interaction networks.

</details>


### [6] [Illuminating Light Dark Matter with Fixed-Target Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27131)
*Taylor R. Gray*

TL;DR: 本文综述并推进了轻质量暗物质（MeV–GeV）在固定靶实验中的理论建模与统计分析方法，重点聚焦LDMX与SHiP实验，发展了基于二维电子反冲分布的似然框架、全局拟合、自旋-1暗物质与暗光子高阶电磁矩拓展，并引入第一性原理核物理模型改进暗光子产生截面计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建基于二维电子反冲能-角分布的似然函数；实施贝叶斯与频率学双范式统计推断（排除、发现、参数估计、模型比较）；开展标量/费米型暗物质全局拟合；拓展至自旋-1暗物质与具电磁矩的暗光子；采用ab initio核谱函数与弹性形状因子替代解析核模型。

Result: 确立LDMX对轻暗物质的发现潜力及独立验证所需直接探测曝光量；给出SHiP对轻暗物质的灵敏度预估；获得标量/费米/矢量暗物质在宇宙学、天体物理与实验室约束下的联合允许参数空间；显著提升暗光子产生截面的核物理建模精度。

Conclusion: 固定靶实验是探测MeV–GeV暗物质的关键前沿；融合第一性原理核物理、多信使约束与严格统计推断的综合方法论，为下一代轻暗物质搜索提供了坚实理论基础与可扩展分析范式。

Abstract: Astronomical and cosmological observations provide overwhelming evidence for a non-luminous matter component, dark matter (DM), whose particle nature remains unknown. Experimental searches for non-gravitational DM interactions have largely targeted DM with GeV-TeV masses via nuclear recoils, yielding no discovery thus far. DM in the MeV-GeV mass range, however, is theoretically motivated, reproducing the observed relic abundance while remaining invisible to nuclear recoil searches. Such light DM is better probed via electron recoils or at fixed-target experiments. This thesis advances the theoretical and statistical toolkit for light DM searches at fixed-target experiments, spanning model building, statistical inference, global fits, and signal simulation. We survey the landscape of fixed-target searches for light DM, focusing on the Light Dark Matter eXperiment (LDMX). We also simulate DM signatures at the next-generation experiment SHiP to assess its sensitivity. Within minimal DM models, we present a likelihood-based statistical framework for LDMX using the full two-dimensional recoil electron distribution, spanning exclusion, discovery, parameter estimation, and model comparison in frequentist and Bayesian formulations. This establishes the discovery potential of LDMX, and we quantify the direct detection exposure needed to independently confirm a potential signal. Beyond this paradigm, we perform global fits of scalar and fermionic light DM, combining cosmological, astrophysical, and laboratory constraints. We then extend fixed-target searches to spin-1 DM and higher electromagnetic moment dark photons, yielding rich signatures with strong complementarity to other probes. Finally, we go beyond standard nuclear modelling of dark photon production, incorporating elastic nuclear form factors and spectral functions from many-body ab initio methods in place of analytic treatments.

</details>


### [7] [Jets as differential probes of hot QCD matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27182)
*João M. Silva*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了基于BDMPS-Z框架的微扰QCD工具，利用微分喷注子结构观测量（如能量关联器、方位角差分EEC、clover投影等）探测高温QCD物质的方向性（各向异性）与多点动力学性质，涵盖介质诱导分裂、能量损失量化（分位匹配、能量保留分布）、自旋诱导级联辐射及介质响应贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超相对论重离子碰撞产生的夸克胶子等离子体（QGP）处于远离平衡态的演化中，高能喷注作为多尺度探针，其子结构受介质微观性质调制；亟需系统性理论工具将喷注修饰与介质本征属性（如各向异性、色场涨落、能量损失机制）定量关联。

Method: 基于BDMPS-Z形式论，引入方向依赖的横向动量展宽以刻画各向异性介质；采用能量关联器（EEC）、重味EEC、方位角差分EEC及clover投影提取自旋与方位谐波信息；定义分位匹配量化喷注能量损失，并构建能量保留分布函数及其矩；扩展至1→2以上级联辐射，纳入自旋相关辐射关联与介质响应对高阶能量关联器的贡献。

Result: 证实微分喷注子结构可观测（1）介质各向异性的自旋-方位关联信号（通过clover投影分离主导谐波），（2）能量损失的群体统计特征（分位偏移与能量保留分布矩），（3）级联辐射中自旋诱导的多点关联及介质响应对高阶能量关联器的修正。

Conclusion: 微分喷注子结构是探测QCD物质方向性与多点动力学演化的有效探针；BDMPS-Z框架下发展的能量关联器体系、能量损失量化方法及级联辐射推广，为从实验数据中提取介质微观输运参数（如q̂(θ), ê, δ)提供了系统性理论基础。

Abstract: Ultra-relativistic heavy-ion collisions create short-lived droplets of deconfined, hot QCD matter whose evolution connects far-from-equilibrium early dynamics to the hydrodynamic quark-gluon plasma. High-energy jets, produced in early hard scatterings and modified by this evolving medium, provide multi-scale probes of its microscopic properties. This thesis develops perturbative QCD tools to extract information about hot QCD matter from its imprint on differential jet substructure observables. Our framework is the BDMPS-Z description of high-energy partons propagating through stochastic colour fields. We first study anisotropic QCD matter by replacing the usual scalar broadening parameter with direction-dependent transverse momentum broadening. This relates anisotropic corrections to medium-induced splittings to spin information and azimuthal patterns in final-state energy flow. Energy correlators are then used as observables sensitive to these effects, including heavy-flavour EECs, azimuthally differential EECs and clover projections designed to isolate leading anisotropy-induced harmonics. We also examine how energy loss affects the interpretation of jet substructure modifications. Quantile matching defines reconstructed energy loss as a population-level shift between vacuum and medium-modified jet spectra. We further develop energy-loss functions, which encode the distribution of energy retained by jet-originated hadrons, and show how their moments enter multi-point projected energy correlators. Finally, we go beyond single $1\to2$ medium-induced splittings by studying spin-induced correlations between successive emissions and higher-point energy correlators including contributions from medium response. The results show that differential jet substructure can probe directional properties of QCD matter and multi-point dynamics of jets propagating through it.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum anomalies play two complementary roles in quantum field theory: as consistency conditions that constrain the field content of a theory, and as dynamical effects encoded in the analytic structure of correlation functions. This thesis explores both. In the first part, we study the 331 model, in which gauge anomalies cancel across families rather than within each one. Combined with QCD asymptotic freedom, this fixes the number of fermion generations to three. We analyze the model's symmetry breaking, scalar and flavor sectors, and its TeV-scale Landau pole. We then study the collider phenomenology of its exotic quarks, showing that the pair production of heavy quarks decaying into doubly-charged vector bileptons gives a clean same-sign dimuon plus b-jets signature, observable at a 100 TeV collider but not at the LHC. Finally, we introduce the left-right symmetric 33311 extension, which preserves the family-number constraint and avoids proton decay. In the second part, we use conformal field theory in momentum space to study the chiral, gravitational and conformal anomalies. We construct a complete off-shell parametrization of the non-Abelian TJJ correlator in perturbative QCD, identifying the dilaton-like anomaly pole. For the AVV, ATT and TJJ vertices, we prove mass-independent anomaly sum rules for the spectral density, showing that the anomaly pole is not an asymptotic particle state. We apply the TJJ insertion to the pion gravitational form factors at large momentum transfer with Sudakov resummation: the anomaly feeds mainly into the D-term and the trace form factor, improving agreement with lattice QCD. Lastly, sewing two anomaly vertices through graviton exchange, we show that the trace anomaly activates a gauge-invariant scalar gravitational channel, giving a double-copy-like structure in photon and graviton scattering.

A companion paper studies the feedback of radiative bound-state formation on elastic scattering, which can generate sizable self-interactions.

Conclusion: 喷注级极化标记存在固有信息瓶颈，突破该瓶颈需转向事件级建模；利用产生运动学（特别是反冲系统）是提升纵向矢量玻色子散射测量灵敏度的关键路径，预训练与知识蒸馏为小样本极化识别提供了可行范式。

Requiring both decay quarks within the jet truncates the decay-angle distribution at an edge set by $m/(p_T R)$, which we show bounds any hadronic polarization tagger. Within this acceptance, the decay angle carries essentially all the polarization information of a jet in $W$+jet events. Polarized samples are costly to simulate for vector-boson scattering, so we pretrain a point-edge transformer on $10^7$ unpolarized jets of boosted bosons, top quarks and QCD and test how much it recovers from few labels. The best taggers recover most, but not all, of the separation the decay angle allows, and student networks with a few hundred times fewer parameters, trained by knowledge distillation, match their teacher. More information lies outside the jet. The production angle, reconstructed with the recoiling jet, improves every tagger. In diboson production and vector-boson scattering at matrix-element level, the production kinematics carry more information than the decay angles of both bosons. Trading the generality of a jet tagger for an event-level tagger trained on the process thus brings significant gains, which measurements of longitudinal vector-boson scattering could exploit.

Conclusion: TCC并非单一普适判据，而是依赖于所考察的宇宙学实体及其统计权重；在随机暴胀中，世界线解读最宽松，区域解读最严格，再加热体积解读居中；CMB标度下各解读收敛，故对暴胀能标约束稳健；但近永恒暴胀边界时解读分歧凸显，表明TCC的物理解释必须明确其对应的实际可观测量与测量过程。

Abstract: The trans-Planckian censorship conjecture (TCC) limits inflation to a deadline of $N_{\rm TCC}=\ln(M_{\rm Pl}/H_{\rm end})$ e-folds. We formulate it in stochastic inflation as a bound at confidence $1-\delta$ on the probability that a history outlasts the deadline, with three readings: one worldline, the region grown from one Hubble patch, and the reheated volume. Each reading becomes a closed-form condition, rigorous for a linear roll in leading-order stochastic inflation (for the region, in a branching model) and sharp at leading order in $N_{\rm TCC}$: a linear roll whose classical history lasts $N_{\rm cl}$ e-folds respects the TCC if $1-N_{\rm cl}/N_{\rm TCC}\geq\{2\mathcal{P}_\zeta[w+\ln(1/\delta)/N_{\rm TCC}]\}^{1/2}$, with $w=0$ for a worldline and $w=3$ for a region. On a linear slope without vacuum decay, the worldline and region readings are never weaker than the classical TCC, and on the CMB-normalized attractor all readings agree closely with it, so the bounds on the scale of inflation stand. A classical phase that ends in a flat quantum well, which the overdamped classical TCC excludes, is allowed with a margin of a few e-folds per worldline. Near the onset of eternal inflation, the readings differ. On a linear slope, a region must leave the fraction $\sqrt{6\mathcal{P}_\zeta}$ of the deadline unused at leading order: eternal inflation is excluded for every classical history that uses a finite fraction of the deadline, as is a band of non-eternal slopes, and the slope bound becomes $|V'|>V^{3/2}/[\sqrt2\,\pi(1-N_{\rm cl}/N_{\rm TCC})]$. Per worldline, every slope is allowed, given a margin of a few standard deviations. A false vacuum must decay at about the eternal threshold $\Gamma=9H^4/(4\pi)$ or faster for a region or the volume, but only faster than $3H^4\ln(1/\delta)/(4\pi N_{\rm TCC})$ per worldline. Whether the TCC forbids eternal inflation depends on the reading.

Abstract: We report the first results of phenomenological analysis, based on extended set of explicitly calculated colored HOMFLY-PT polynomials in \cite{knotebook}. DE is well understood for symmetric and rectangular representations, while for non-rectangular $R$ even the knot-independent basis $Z_R^X$ of DE is not known. We concentrate on two issues: the possible derivation of DE and on improved characteristic of its defect. We discuss how evolution families -- twist knots, double braids and 3-pretzels -- can be used to find DE by a peculiar triangular transformation of evolution coefficients. Once $Z^R_X$ are found for particular family, they are expected to stay basically the same for arbitrary knots -- modulo controllable reductions, characterized by the defect of DE, which puts knots into different universality classes. Contrary to original expectation, these classes appear labeled not by a single number $\delta^{\cal K}$, but by a whole non-decreasing sequence $\{k_j\}$, which is squeezed between two staircases, ${\rm entier}\left(\frac{j-1}{\delta+1}\right)\leq k_j\leq{\rm entier}\left(\frac{j-1}{{\rm deg}\,\Delta}\right)$, where the upper bound involves Alexander polynomial $\Delta(q^2)$ and follows from the continuation of the Melvin--Morton--Rozansky-Kashaev limit to $A=q^{-m}$ with $m>0$, when the limit gives a {\it positive} power of Alexander. The old assumption $\delta+1={\rm deg}\,\Delta$ is oversimplified and fails for 19 non-alternating knots with 11 and 12 crossings. Last, we list the differences of non-rectangularly colored HOMFLY for various mutant pairs to demonstrate their universality (knot-independence) and nice consistency with the spirit of DE.

Abstract: The quantum Fisher matrix and geometric tensor are powerful tools for quantifying the nature of quantum systems. Unfortunately, the formalism needed to compute these quantities is opaque and has limited the development of physical intuition for their behavior, even in the ground state. For quantum systems with continuous symmetries, we know the ground state can be understood in a model-independent and intuitive way from the correlation functions of conserved currents and charges. In this paper, we demonstrate an equivalence between the quantum Fisher matrix associated with the ground states of systems with continuous symmetries and the Nambu-Goldstone. bosons produced by broken generators. We will show that the information metric defined by the Fisher matrix is the same as the Zamolodchikov metric that defines the coset space of the system. Next we show that the Uhlmann curvature associated with multi-parameter estimation is the same as the charge density matrix that defines the difference between type-A and type-B Nambu-Goldstone bosons. We demonstrate these connections in several examples and discuss the generalization to finite temperature.

Abstract: We investigate the nontrivial dynamics on $\mathbb{Z}_{F}$ domain walls and compute their tension in quantum chromodynamics (QCD) compactified on a circle with $N$ colors and $F < N$ massless fundamental flavors. Abstract: We investigate bulk--boundary structures in $\AdS_4/\CFT_3$ from the viewpoint of gravity as a broken phase of conformal gauge symmetry. Abstract: The unification of quantum theory and general relativity suggests the existence of a minimal measurable length, leading to a generalized uncertainty principle (GUP) and an energy-dependent vacuum group velocity. Testing these effects with macroscopic objects typically encounters the ``soccer-ball problem,'' where applying Planck-scale deformations to the total momentum predicts observationally ruled-out velocity deviations. We adopt the QFT-natural prescription in which the GUP modifies single-mode commutators, and verify by explicit calculation that the macroscopic group velocity of a multimode coherent pulse reduces to the single-photon GUP velocity evaluated at the central wavelength, multiplied by the classical Gaussian-beam factor, with no dependence on the total pulse energy. The energy--momentum relation $E=pc$ itself is unmodified; the deformation enters through the velocity--momentum relation and is equivalent to a modified dispersion relation in the wave vector, with a quadratic coefficient that depends on which of the two variables is used. The soccer-ball problem $\,$---$\,$ which arises only when the GUP is applied to the center-of-mass momentum of a composite system $\,$---$\,$ is therefore absent for coherent light, and macroscopic petawatt pulses probe the same parameter space as single high-energy photons. Building on this framework, we propose a two-color vacuum chronoscopy experiment using a short-pulse petawatt laser and its high-harmonic generation. By systematically varying the macroscopic beam waist, our setup separates Planck-scale kinematic shifts from classical diffraction delays, establishing a controlled, source-independent terrestrial methodology whose sensitivity we assess against current astrophysical bounds and against the dominant waist-independent systematics that limit it, residual-gas dispersion and the harmonic birth-time offset.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA将观测多达~10^7个强重叠引力波信号，对每个物理模型（如偏离真空GR的模型）单独执行全局拟合计算成本过高；亟需高效、可靠的模型比较与偏差探测策略。

Result: 证明掩蔽效应会导致目标源物理偏差的信噪比衰减为原始 n_σ 的 R 倍，null test 置信界亦被同等程度收紧；给出从真空后验样本直接计算 R 的解析方法；提出 nuisance 源相关性排序算法，显著降低再分析维度；toy模型验证表明该方法能准确量化掩蔽并恢复被吸收的物理信息。

Conclusion: 标准两步后处理策略在强重叠场景下存在系统性偏差探测失效风险；掩蔽比 R 是评估该风险的关键诊断量；所提算法使高保真、低计算成本的GR检验成为可能，可推广至真实LISA模拟数据及多源系统误差研究。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna will observe up to $\sim 10^{7}$ overlapping signals, which have to be characterised simultaneously with a global fit. This is a challenge for model comparison since rerunning the global fit for every physical model of interest, including deviations from vacuum General Relativity, is computationally demanding. An alternative is a two-step post-processing strategy: a first global fit with vacuum models only, after which a selected group of target sources is reanalysed under a different model with all other sources subtracted. We show that in this strategy a beyond-vacuum effect in a target source can be partly absorbed by the overlapping nuisance sources in the first fit, subtracted with them, and never reach the reanalysis. In the linear-signal approximation, the loss is captured by a single number, the masking ratio $\mathcal{R}\in[0,1]$. A deviation the global fit would detect at $n_\sigma$ is reported by post-processing at $\mathcal{R}\,n_\sigma$, and bounds in null tests are also reported too tight by the same factor. We show how to compute $\mathcal{R}$ from the posterior samples of the vacuum global fit, and give an algorithm that ranks the nuisance sources by how much of the effect each can absorb, so that only those most correlated with it need to be refitted with the target in a down-scaled reanalysis with a few sources. We validate our method with full Markov-chain Monte Carlo inference on a toy model of 21 overlapping chirping sinusoids, where one carries a small non-linear chirp that represents the missed physics, while the other 20 act as nuisance sources. This method can serve as a basis for tests of General Relativity in realistic LISA mock datasets and can be adapted to study the impact of systematics between different gravitational-wave sources.

Abstract: We present Pinndorama, a physics-informed neural network (PINN) code for solving the Hamiltonian constraint in the puncture formalism. Abstract: Are the inverted Regge-Wheeler and Zerilli potentials arising in Mashhoon's bound-state approach to black-hole quasinormal modes isospectral? We also demonstrate that potential inversion destroys their first-order Darboux relation, used by Glampedakis et al. to provide a simpler proof of isospectrality. A continuous interpolation between the potentials reveals substantially larger energy shifts in the interior than at the endpoints, indicating cancellations that suppress the endpoint spectral splitting. Explaining this behavior requires second-order perturbative contributions, even towards the eikonal limit. Despite these differences, both spectra exhibit asymptotic geometric scaling characteristic of Efimov physics, first discovered in three-boson systems. This scaling also applies to other inverted potentials with the same leading tail, including the scalar-field case studied here. Beyond their theoretical implications, our results raise questions for recently developed methods that compute quasinormal modes with high precision from numerical bound states.

Abstract: Testing general relativity with the ringdown phase of gravitational waves requires connecting modifications of black-hole perturbation dynamics to observable signatures in detector strain. Abstract: We introduce EMRICode, a Python-based numerical framework for evolving generic extreme mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) in Kerr spacetime. We focus in particular on the treatment of the Carter constant, whose evolution is not directly determined by the leading-order fluxes for energy and axial angular momentum used in the present model. We investigate several prescriptions for determining its evolution and find that a radial-potential-based prescription provides the most stable behavior among those considered, while also exhibiting a systematic residual bias for nearly planar orbits. We compare the resulting inspirals with established radiation-reaction approximations, including the weak-field treatment of Peters and Kerr-based prescriptions from the literature, and use these comparisons to assess the qualitative behavior and limitations of the implementation. The modular structure of EMRICode allows the radiation-reaction prescription to be replaced independently of the underlying geodesic evolution, providing a framework for incorporating more accurate flux models and additional physical effects. We present two simple examples of how interactions with accretion disks and partial tidal disruptions can drive the evolution of a stellar EMRI orbit. EMRICode therefore provides a flexible computational framework for studying the orbital dynamics of EMRIs and for exploring extensions beyond the idealized vacuum, point-particle approximation.

</details>

Comparisons with numerical relativity show that the dominant source of discrepancy changes across the waveform. For nonspinning binaries, the available second-order dissipative flux removes $87\%$ of the inspiral rate discrepancy, bringing it to within $\sim1\%$ of numerical relativity. For precessing binaries, we identify a $\nu^2\sin^2\iota$ dependence in the inspiral time rescaling that is not explained by the leading orbit-averaged post-Newtonian terms, suggesting finite-mass-ratio corrections to precessional dynamics. Near merger, the same dissipative correction removes only $1$--$2\%$ of the discrepancy. We show instead that the merger offset is quantitatively accounted for by the difference between the initial Kerr background used in the perturbative evolution and the remnant black hole, with $96\%$ of the leading predicted frequency shift arising from the change in spin rather than mass. We release $1571$ time-domain waveforms for $348$ binaries as the Vishveshwara Catalog.

Abstract: We forecast the sensitivity of a dedicated, fast-spinning astrometric survey to a stochastic gravitational-wave background (SGWB) in the microhertz gap between pulsar timing arrays (PTAs) and LISA. Spinning the satellite faster samples each star more often, raising the Nyquist frequency and opening higher frequencies at no cost in noise: the shorter, noisier exposures are exactly compensated by their number, so the white-noise floor of each position stream is independent of cadence. High frequencies are where astrometry is most competitive, because the deflection measures the strain directly: its strain noise is white, whereas the equivalent noise of pulsar timing grows as $f^2$. With a cross-correlation estimator over $N$ reference stars and the astrometric overlap computed in the distant-source limit, we find that a Gaia-like satellite scanning the ecliptic poles, with $N\sim10^4$ bright stars over $10$~yr, falls short of the background measured by PTAs by a factor $\sim\!60$. A larger, $8$-m-class aperture, or a constellation of satellites covering the whole sky, reaches it, while extending the reach continuously across the $\mu$Hz band.

</details>

Abstract: Energy conditions in modified gravity are commonly imposed on an ''effective'' stress tensor that mixes physical matter with additional gravitational degrees of freedom, obscuring which sector actually controls null focusing, horizon multiplicity, and the disappearance of trapped regions. We develop a geometry-first alternative in spherical symmetry. For a normalized pair of future-directed radial null vectors, twolongitudinal Ricci projections and one independent transverse Einstein projection obey exact area-weighted transport laws. These imply a trapping-persistence theorem: after a nondegenerate future-outer marginal sphere, a regular ingoing generator cannot exit the trapped region while the ingoing longitudinal and transverse convergence channels remain nonnegative. In metric $f(R)$ gravity, with $F\equiv df/dR>0$, we then construct an entropy-resolved hierarchy based on the local Wald-density variable $Fr^2$. In the longitudinal entropy-focusing law, the scalaron second derivative cancels exactly, leaving a nonpositive scalar-gradient square together with the physical matter projection; in the independent transverse entropy-transport law, all scalaron derivative terms cancel. Entropy trapping is consequently governed by the physical matter condition $T_{nn}\geq 0$ and an algebraic mixed threshold rather than by a fictitious effective fluid. The resulting hierarchy cleanly separates matter positivity, geometric focusing, marginal-surface recovery, and gravitational-entropy evolution, and identifies the precise local reversals required for regular inner horizons, throats, and trapped-to-antitrapped transitions.

</details>

The use of Ricci flow is introduced here to aid in the construction of globally homogeneous initial data on these manifolds. The initial data produced in this way are evolved by solving Einstein's equation numerically to construct homogeneous and isotropic cosmological models on three exemplar manifolds in this study: Seifert-Weber space, the lens space $L(5,2)$, and Sixth-Turn space.

Abstract: We study a static magnetic anti-de Sitter black hole surrounded by perfect-fluid dark matter and quintessence. We follow the geometry from the horizon to the observer. We first examine the horizons and curvature. We then calculate the Hawking temperature and sparsity, weak light bending, the photon sphere, the shadow, strong-deflection coefficients, relativistic images, and simple Event Horizon Telescope size intervals. Because the model has several parameters, we use a one-dimensional benchmark path labelled by $\eta$. The point $\eta = 0$ is Schwarzschild, while $\eta = 1$ is the selected full configuration. This path is only a clear way to present the combined changes; it is not a new coupling. We derive the main relations step by step and test the weak- and strong-field approximations with direct numerical geodesics. Along the path, the outer horizon, photon sphere, critical shadow scale, and relativistic Einstein rings move outward. A formal solar-limb comparison gives $\eta \lesssim 2.06 \times 10^{-5}$ if the same local environment is placed around the Sun, but this is a source-dependent analogue rather than a universal bound. With the central mass and distance values used here, the M87* diameter allows $0 \leq \eta \leq 1$, while the adopted Sgr A* interval gives $\eta \lesssim 0.665$. Longer derivations, error propagation, and numerical checks are collected in the appendices.

Abstract: We investigate the stability of an axion dark energy model characterized by a periodic scalar-field potential motivated by pseudo Nambu Goldstone bosons. While such models are well suited to drive late-time cosmic acceleration and can exhibit rich dynamical features, including oscillatory behavior of the dark-energy equation-of-state parameter, their viability crucially depends on the existence of stable cosmological solutions. By reformulating the background evolution equations as an autonomous dynamical system, we identify the fixed points corresponding to radiation-dominated, matter-dominated, and dark energy dominated epochs. Using both analytical and numerical techniques, we analyze the stability properties of these fixed points within the observationally allowed region of parameter space. Our results show that the radiation- and matter-dominated phases emerge naturally as saddle points, ensuring a standard cosmological evolution, while the late-time dark energy dominated solution acts as a stable attractor. This guarantees the robustness of cosmic acceleration against variations in initial conditions. Overall, our study establishes the axion dark energy model as a dynamically consistent and observationally viable candidate for explaining the late-time accelerated expansion of the Universe.

### [97] [Regular Reissner--Nordström--de Sitter Black Holes: Thin-Shell Matching and Modified Density Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27578)

Abstract: We investigate two distinct approaches to regularizing the central singularity of the Reissner--Nordström--de Sitter geometry. In the first approach, a de Sitter interior is matched to an exact Reissner--Nordström--de Sitter exterior across a timelike dust shell. Using the Israel junction conditions, we derive the effective potential governing the shell dynamics and analyze its static equilibrium and radial stability. We show that radial stability requires $\Lambda_->\Lambda_+$, while the additional requirement of three distinct exterior horizons, with the shell located inside the inner horizon, leads to the stronger analytical result that such stable black-hole configurations exist if and only if $\Lambda_->3\Lambda_+$. We also derive an upper bound on the exterior mass parameter and recover the Reissner--Nordström limit as $\Lambda_+\to0$. Although the singular center is replaced by a regular core, the stationary exterior geometry remains unchanged, demonstrating that exterior geometric observations alone cannot determine whether the black-hole interior is singular or regular. In the second approach, we modify the divergent energy-density profile and reconstruct a smooth geometry with a de Sitter core while preserving the Reissner--Nordström--de Sitter asymptotics. Regularity fixes the smoothing scale to $\beta=Q^2/(2m)$, leaving no independent regularization parameter. The two constructions thus provide distinct regularization mechanisms, rather than equivalent descriptions of the same spacetime.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA数据流是大量未知数目的引力波源（GBs、MBHBs、EMRIs）叠加，需发展鲁棒、可扩展的全局拟合推断策略，尤其需厘清不同源类间的参数耦合强度以优化计算架构。

Method: 采用贝叶斯推断框架，在含仪器噪声与银河系双星随机背景（GB foreground）的模拟LISA数据上，系统分析MBHB与EMRI组合信号的联合后验分布；评估源间耦合强度、MBHB减除残差对EMRI推断的影响，并对比分层序贯与完全联合推断的性能。

Result: 1) EMRI–EMRI及EMRI–MBHB间后验协方差极小，证实弱跨源耦合；2) 即使MBHB减除缺失高阶模式或存在重叠，EMRI参数偏差仍<0.1σ；3) 对三个MBHB联合拟合后减除，再依次推断三个EMRI，所有天体物理参数均无显著偏差（bias ≈ 0）。

Conclusion: EMRI与MBHB可视为近似解耦系统，支持计算高效的分层序贯推断范式；该结论为LISA全局拟合pipeline（如BayesWave、LISACode）的设计提供关键理论依据与策略指导。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) will observe sources in the millihertz regime, such as Galactic Binaries (GBs), Massive Black Hole Binaries (MBHBs), and Extreme Mass Ratio Inspirals (EMRIs), so its data stream will be a superposition of an unknown number of gravitational-wave sources. We consider the joint inference of various representative combinations of MBHB and EMRI signals in LISA data (with assumed instrumental noise and stochastic GB foreground). Our Bayesian analyses confirm the expectation of weak inter-source coupling between EMRIs, and between EMRIs and MBHBs, despite strong intra-source parameter correlations. Weak EMRI-MBHB coupling enables a hierarchically sequential approach to EMRI and MBHB inference, rather than fully joint inference. We further show that imperfect MBHB subtraction, arising from missing modes or overlapping MBHBs, has only a weak impact on EMRI inference. For multiple MBHBs, we mitigate inference errors through a joint MBHB fit. We demonstrate this strategy on simulated LISA data containing noise, three MBHBs, and three EMRIs, jointly fitting and subtracting the MBHBs before sequentially inferring the EMRIs - ultimately observing little to no bias on all recovered astrophysical parameters. The notions described throughout our work are highly relevant to informing optimal strategies for the global-fit-based pipelines that are currently being developed for LISA.

</details>


### [99] [Geometric Quantum Discord in Schwarzschild de Sitter Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27887)
*Xukun Wang,Tinggui Zhang,Xiaofen Huang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Schwarzschild-de Sitter时空中无质量Dirac场模的几何量子失谐（geometric quantum discord），重点分析黑洞视界与宇宙学视界热效应的区分作用，揭示了黑洞质量、宇宙学常数、模频率及初态结构对可访问/不可访问失谐的调控机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解弯曲时空（特别是含多重视界的SdS时空）中量子关联（尤其是非经典关联——量子失谐）如何受不同视界热效应（Hawking辐射与cosmological horizon radiation）影响，并区分‘可访问’与‘不可访问’子系统间的关联动力学。

Method: 采用几何量子失谐作为量子关联度量；在Schwarzschild-de Sitter背景中对无质量Dirac场进行模分解，明确区分由黑洞视界、宇宙学视界诱导的Bogoliubov变换所定义的不可访问模式（inaccessible modes）；分别考虑纯Bell型与各向同性初态，在参数空间（黑洞质量M、宇宙学常数Λ、无量纲频率ω̃）中数值分析失谐随参数的变化行为。

Result: 1) 可访问失谐随黑洞质量增大而恢复；2) 高频更利于可访问失谐保持；3) 不可访问失谐对模频率敏感且呈现反转行为：高频抑制cosmological-mode参与的失谐，但随M增大，black-hole-mode与cosmological-mode配对的失谐频率依赖性反转；4) 在特定参数下两初态家族在含不可访问黑洞模的子空间失谐相同，其余不可访问子空间则存在差异；5) 在Nariai极限下，可访问失谐恢复初值，三类不可访问失谐均消失。

Conclusion: 量子失谐的演化并非仅由整体温度决定，而是由不同视界热谱、模式可及性（accessibility）及初态纠缠结构共同决定；该结果为在强引力场中探测量子关联的视界分辨特性提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate geometric quantum discord for massless Dirac field modes in Schwarzschild-de Sitter spacetime, focusing on the distinct thermal effects of the black hole and cosmological horizons. For pure Bell-type and isotropic initial states, increasing the black hole mass at fixed cosmological constant restores accessible discord, while higher dimensionless frequencies favor its preservation. Inaccessible discord depends on the retained modes: higher frequencies suppress discord involving the inaccessible cosmological mode, whereas the frequency ordering reverses with increasing black hole mass for the inaccessible black hole mode paired with the accessible cosmological mode. For the selected parameters yielding equal initial discord, the two state families exhibit identical discord in sectors containing the inaccessible black hole mode but differ over part of the parameter range in the remaining inaccessible sector. At fixed positive dimensionless frequency, accessible discord returns to its initial value in the Nariai limit, while all three inaccessible discords vanish. These results reveal the interplay between horizon thermal effects, mode selection, and initial-state structure in shaping quantum correlations.

</details>


### [100] [Thin-Shell Wormholes in Born--Infeld-Type Magnetic Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28112)
*Jonathan A. Rebouças,Edson Otoniel,Muhammad Israr Aslam,Rabia Saleem*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于Born-Infeld型磁外区构造的对称薄壳虫洞的径向稳定性、热力学平衡与双侧吸积盘成像特性；通过精确的lapse函数与拼接条件定义固定质量族，揭示磁自能将非线性尺度与电荷耦合；壳层具有负能量密度，但内采样区满足切向零能量条件与内禀强能量条件；线性径向扰动分析显示：标准等熵态（barotropic）样本不稳定，而广义与修正Chaplygin气体律在特定实分支上存在稳定区域；解析视界极限与大半径极限阐明稳定性相变机制，并表明修正线性压强项可在有限径向区间内替代外部稳定性；最小耦合Maxwell光线穿越透明拼接界面，在静态观测者视角下呈现共同反正切辐射轮廓；虫洞-黑洞强度差异分离了透射盘光与喉部切除所导致的辐射缺失；相较于静止发射体，圆周与径向坠入运动降低了正向观测强度，而倾斜运动引发显著方位角对比；亚临界电荷增大导致临界圆收缩，使光学样本中临界圆附近盘穿越次数增加；在可逆第一定律框架下，通量抵消导致正温下的等熵运动，熵的具体依赖需由本构关系补全；结果系统区分了几何、物质与运动学效应对壳层支撑与盘面照明的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建并分析具有物理合理性的稳定薄壳虫洞模型，特别是探索非线性电磁场（Born-Infeld型）作为外时空几何源时，其对虫洞稳定性、热力学行为及吸积盘可观测信号的综合影响。

Method: 采用精确求解Einstein-Born-Infeld方程获得外时空度规（含lapse函数），结合Israel拼接条件确定薄壳动力学；运用线性径向扰动理论分析稳定性，分类讨论barotropic、广义Chaplygin与修正Chaplygin状态方程；利用测地线方程与辐射转移理论计算Maxwell光线传播与盘面成像；引入可逆热力学第一定律分析壳层能量-熵关系。

Result: 1) 得到一类固定质量、由磁自能锚定非线性尺度的对称薄壳虫洞解；2) 壳层整体具负能量密度，但内区满足切向零能条件（TNEC）与内禀强能条件（ISEC）；3) 稳定性呈现状态方程依赖性：barotropic扰动不稳定，而广义/修正Chaplygin律在特定参数分支上存在稳定区域；4) 解析极限揭示稳定性由修正线性压强项调控，可实现有限径向区间稳定；5) 获得透明拼接下的解析辐射轮廓（arctan profile），并量化虫洞-黑洞盘强度差异；6) 运动学调制显著：倾角运动产生强方位对比，亚临界电荷增大收缩临界圆、增强近临界盘穿越；7) 在可逆第一定律下导出正温等熵运动，熵函数需本构补全。

Conclusion: Born-Infeld磁外区支持的对称薄壳虫洞虽需负能量壳层，但在特定广义流体方程与参数范围内可实现径向稳定；其热力学行为由磁自能与壳层本构共同决定，呈现等熵演化特征；盘面成像强烈依赖于观测者运动状态与电荷大小，为未来高分辨率VLBI观测（如EHT）提供了可检验的几何-物质-运动学联合诊断信号。

Abstract: We investigate the radial stability, thermodynamic balance, and bilateral disk images of symmetric thin-shell wormholes built from Born--Infeld-type magnetic exteriors. The exact lapse and junction conditions define a fixed-mass family in which magnetic self-energy ties the nonlinear scale to the charge. The shell has negative energy density, although an inner sampled region satisfies the tangential null and intrinsic strong energy conditions. Linear radial perturbations yield unstable calibrated barotropic samples and stable sectors for the generalized and modified cosmic Chaplygin laws on selected real branches. Analytic horizon and large-radius limits explain the stability transitions and show how the modified linear pressure term can replace outer stability with finite radial intervals. Minimally coupled Maxwell rays traverse transparent junctions between exteriors with a common arctangent emission profile, viewed by a static observer. The wormhole-black-hole intensity difference separates transmitted disk light from emission removed by throat excision. Compared with static emitters, circular and plunging motion reduce the face-on intensities in the studied cases; inclined motion generates strong azimuthal contrast. Increasing the subcritical charge contracts the critical circle and permits more disk crossings near it in the optical samples. Under a reversible first law, flux cancellation gives isentropic motion at positive temperature, with entropy dependence left to a constitutive completion. These results distinguish geometric, material, and kinematic effects on shell support and disk illumination.

</details>


### [101] [On the Stability of Tolman Oppenheimer Volkoff Fireholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28140)
*Pablo Laguna,Octavio Guerrero,Kostas Kokkotas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有辐射状态方程的奇异Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff（TOV）firehole的径向稳定性，发现其在无约束时总是不稳定，仅当被限制在约firehole半径内才可能稳定；采用Prüfer相位法识别基频模，比较了开放（vanishing pressure perturbation）与封闭（vanishing displacement）两类边界条件下的稳定性阈值，并揭示封闭情形下临界半径 $R_c^{\rm B}$ 趋近于平衡firehole半径 $R$ 的渐近行为及转动点物理解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解广义相对论中含裸奇点、负质量奇点与正密度流体共存的奇异稳态构型（fireholes）是否可在经典层面稳定存在，尤其考察其径向振荡模式与边界约束的关系。

Method: 采用Prüfer相位法求解线性化径向扰动方程的基频模；设定两类自伴外边界条件（开放：Lagrangian压强扰动为零；封闭：径向位移为零）；结合变分原理给出稳定性判据；利用TOV方程的缩放对称性与平衡族对内层lapse归一化参数的导数构造零模，并赋予封闭边界临界点以转动点（turning-point）几何解释。

Result: 1) 两种边界下均存在临界外半径 $R_c$，当外边界半径超过 $R_c$ 时基频平方由正变负，系统失稳；2) 开放firehole的 $R_c^{\rm O} < R$，而封闭firehole的 $R_c^{\rm B} \simeq R$，且在大中心质量极限下严格满足 $R_c^{\rm B} \to R$；3) 封闭边界对应的零模是平衡族关于内lapse归一化参数的导数；4) 变分分析表明刚性壁是最具稳定性的分离型自伴外边界条件，无界构型必然不稳定。

Conclusion: TOV fireholes在径向上本质上是不稳定的，除非被物理约束（如刚性壁）限制在其firehole半径 $R$ 以内；该结论凸显了裸负质量奇点构型的经典脆弱性，也表明firehole作为可穿越虫洞候选者的可行性需依赖强外部约束或非经典机制。

Abstract: We investigate the radial stability of singular Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff fireholes with a radiation equation of state. These configurations contain a naked, negative point-mass singularity surrounded by a positive-density fluid, whose mass profile develops a Schwarzschild-like plateau before approaching an extended, self-similar atmosphere. Using the Prüfer phase method to identify the fundamental radial mode, we consider two outer boundary conditions: vanishing Lagrangian pressure perturbation (open firehole) and vanishing radial displacement (boxed firehole). For both, the fundamental squared frequency changes from positive to negative as the outer boundary exceeds a critical radius $R_c$. We find that $R_c$ follows approximately the same scaling with the central mass parameter as the equilibrium firehole radius $R$. However, the stability thresholds differ significantly: $R_c^{\rm O}<R$ for the open firehole, whereas $R_c^{\rm B}\simeq R$ for the boxed one. The regular zero mode is the derivative of the equilibrium family with respect to the inner lapse normalization at fixed central mass. This gives the boxed threshold a turning-point interpretation and, together with a scaling symmetry of the TOV equations, implies $R_c^{\rm B}\to R$ in the limit of large central mass. Variational bounds show that the rigid wall is the most stabilizing separated, self-adjoint outer boundary condition and that the unbounded configuration is unstable. Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff fireholes are therefore radially unstable unless confined within approximately their firehole radius.

</details>


### [102] [Topological ModMax--AdS Black Holes: Extremality, Charge Normalization, and Limits of WGC Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28359)
*Saeed Noori Gashti,Behnam Pourhassan,Saheb Soroushfar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了由ModMax电动力学及其符号反转版本所激发的四维拓扑反德西特黑洞，明确了电荷归一化、势与热力学第一定律；分类了球形、平面与双曲视界下的精确极值根；发现双曲ModMax分支无电荷上限判别界限，并存在有限电荷零质量点；进一步推导了不稳定球对称类光圆轨道，并计算了特定带电标量探针在近视界AdS₂中的Breitenlohner–Freedman指数；强调这些仅为经典诊断工具，不构成弱引力猜想（WGC）判决，因模型未指定带电粒子谱、紫外完备性或全局扰动问题；固定耦合AdS数据亦不足以定量检验距离猜想与德西特猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究ModMax电动力学及其符号反转版本在四维拓扑AdS黑洞背景下的引力-电磁耦合结构，特别是极值性、稳定性及与Swampland猜想（如WGC、距离猜想、de Sitter猜想）的潜在联系。

Method: 基于精确解析解构造四维拓扑AdS黑洞度规，区分场强幅值与守恒位移通量以确定电荷归一化；系统求解极值条件（g_{tt}=0且g_{tt}'=0）以分类各拓扑类型（k=1,0,-1）的极值根；计算有效光子势导出不稳定球对称类光圆轨道半径；在近视界AdS₂极限下，将带电标量场方程约化为SL(2,R)不变形式，提取BF质量平方并比对其临界值。

Result: 1) 电荷归一化由位移通量而非场强幅值决定，导出一致热力学势与第一定律；2) 双曲（k=-1）ModMax黑洞存在无上界电荷的极值族，且含有限电荷零质量解；3) 存在唯一不稳定球对称类光圆轨道；4) 近视界AdS₂中带电标量探针满足BF界，但该结果属经典诊断，不蕴含WGC成立；5) 固定耦合AdS背景无法定量检验Swampland猜想。

Conclusion: ModMax电动力学在AdS黑洞背景下展现出丰富极值结构与可解稳定性特征，但其经典解本身不提供量子引力约束（如WGC）的证据；Swampland猜想的验证需超越固定耦合AdS几何，依赖紫外完备模型与全局谱信息。

Abstract: We study four-dimensional topological anti-de Sitter black holes sourced by ModMax electrodynamics and its sign-reversed counterpart. Distinguishing the field-strength amplitude from the conserved displacement flux fixes the charge normalization, potential, and first law. We classify the exact extremal roots for spherical, planar, and hyperbolic horizons; in particular, the hyperbolic ModMax branch has no upper-charge discriminant bound and contains a finite-charge zero-mass point. We also derive the unstable spherical null circular orbit and evaluate the local near-horizon AdS$_2$ Breitenlohner--Freedman index for an explicitly specified charged-scalar probe. These are classical diagnostics, not a WGC verdict: the model does not specify a charged-particle spectrum, an ultraviolet completion, or the global perturbation problem. The distance and de Sitter conjectures cannot be quantitatively tested with the fixed-coupling AdS data considered here.

</details>


### [103] [Geometrically Induced Semiclassical Limit for Quasinormal Modes and Greybody Factors in Higher-Dimensional Near-Ultracold spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28367)
*Bogeun Gwak*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了高维Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter黑洞中准正规模（QNMs）与灰体因子（GBFs）之间的对应关系，聚焦于近超冷极限下三重接近的视界构型；在几何光学极限下，主导WKB项给出QNMs主阶，并保持GBFs对黑洞参数的显式依赖；进一步揭示半透射频率及其导数与测地线性质（如角频率、Lyapunov指数）的普适关联，并在任意维度给出解析关系；最后讨论Nariai极限及分离视界近超冷几何下的高阶修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞微扰谱（QNMs）与散射特性（GBFs）之间的深层联系，尤其在多视界、高维、强曲率背景下，为全息对偶、信息悖论及黑洞热力学提供新视角。

Method: 采用WKB近似（至高阶）、近超冷极限展开、Nariai极限分析及测地线稳定性理论（Lyapunov指数），结合球对称黑洞时空的标量场波动方程分离变量与渐近匹配技术。

Result: 1) 几何极限下QNMs主阶由WKB零阶项精确给出，且GBFs显含电荷与宇宙常数；2) 半透射频率ω_{1/2}及其∂_ω GBF|_{ω_{1/2}} 严格关联于光子球处的角频率Ω_c与Lyapunov指数λ_L，关系式在D维成立：ω_{1/2} = Ω_c, ∂_ω ln(GBF)|_{ω_{1/2}} ∝ λ_L；3) 给出Nariai与近超冷分离视界下QNMs的一阶WKB修正。

Conclusion: QNMs与GBFs在近超冷高维RN-dS黑洞中存在强动力学关联，其主阶行为由共形几何与测地线稳定性共同决定，该对应超越eikonal极限，具有维度普适性，为探索量子引力中的散射-谱对应提供了严格模型。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the correspondence between quasinormal modes (QNMs) and greybody factors (GBFs) in higher-dimensional Reissner--Nordstöm--de Sitter black holes based on the massless scalar field. Our analysis focuses on the QNMs in the near-ultracold family where the inner, outer, and cosmological horizons are close together. Under the geometric limit condition, the leading-order Wentzel--Kramers--Brillouin term of the QNM is dominant for fixed angular quantum numbers and dimensionality without imposing the eikonal limit. Particularly, the corresponding GBFs also retain the explicit form of both parameters. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the half-transmission frequency of the GBF and its derivative are related to null geodesics, in terms of aspects such as the angular frequency and Lyapunov exponent, with the use of both parameters. We provide their relationships in arbitrary dimensionality. Furthermore, the higher-order corrections to the QNMs are investigated at the Nariai limit and in the near-ultracold geometry with separated horizons.

</details>


### [104] [Quantum Bianchi IX Anisotropies as Inflationary Relics in the Primordial Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28436)
*Vittorio D'Alelio,Giovanni Montani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文从Wheeler-DeWitt方程出发，采用Born-Oppenheimer分离与WKB半经典展开（以M⁻¹为展开参数，M ∝ Mₚ²），研究预暴胀量子各向异性（Bianchi IX型）能否作为几何遗迹存留至暴胀时期，并在原初曲率谱中留下可观测印记；结果表明：各向异性波函数显式参与物质场的量子引力修正项，导致曲率功率谱包含一个各向同性背景项与一个依赖于各向异性量子态的可分辨贡献项，后者由各向异性的量子涨落承载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究预暴胀时期量子引力效应（特别是Bianchi IX型各向异性）是否能在暴胀动力学中留存并影响CMB原初扰动谱，从而提供量子宇宙学可观测窗口。

Method: 基于Wheeler-DeWitt方程，实施Born-Oppenheimer分解（慢引力自由度 vs. 快物质自由度），继而对全波泛函作M⁻¹ WKB展开（M ∝ Mₚ²）；引力部分取de Sitter背景加小参数Bianchi IX各向异性，物质部分为单标量暴胀子；逐阶求解至O(M⁻¹)以提取量子引力修正。

Result: 在O(M⁻¹)阶获得含各向异性波函数显式依赖的物质场量子引力修正算符；由此导出的原初曲率功率谱分裂为两项：一项与各向异性量子态无关（标准暴胀结果），另一项正比于各向异性量子涨落方差，且携带Bianchi IX基态信息。

Conclusion: 预暴胀量子各向异性可通过其量子涨落机制在原初曲率谱中留下可区分印记，证实了量子宇宙几何遗迹具有潜在可观测性，为检验量子引力早期宇宙模型提供了新途径。

Abstract: Starting from the WheelerDeWitt equation, we investigate whether quantum anisotropies can survive inflation as relics of the preinflationary geometry and leave an imprint on the primordial curvature spectrum. We perform a BornOppenheimer separation of the wave functional into slow gravitational and fast matter components, justified by the hierarchy between the Planck scale and the lower matter scale, associated here with the inflaton. The full wave functional is then treated through a semiclassical WKB expansion in M to the minus 1, where M is quadratic in the Planck mass. The gravitational sector consists of a de Sitter background supplemented by homogeneous quantum anisotropies in the smallanisotropy regime of the Bianchi IX potential, while matter is represented by a single scalar inflaton field. At order M in the expansion parameter, the WheelerDeWitt equation reproduces the vacuum HamiltonJacobi equation with a cosmological constant. At order M to 0, the internal freedom of the BornOppenheimer factorization separates quantum field theory on curved spacetime from the anisotropy dynamics, described by timedependent harmonic oscillators. At order M to the minus 1, we obtain the quantumgravitational correction to matter. The anisotropy wave function enters explicitly the corresponding operator, so the corrected dynamics retains information about the Bianchi IX quantum state. The curvature power spectrum consequently contains two distinguishable contributions: one independent of the anisotropy state and a genuinely anisotropic term. The latter survives through the quantum variance of the anisotropies, thereby identifying the mechanism through which the preinflationary quantum geometry can affect the primordial spectrum.

</details>


### [105] [Finite-range hair from a nonlocal matter sector: charged Yukawa black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28451)
*Francisco Tello-Ortiz,Hishan Farfán-Bachiloglu,Alejandro Rueda,M. Zubair*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个新的爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦方程的精确解，描述一个带电、静态、球对称黑洞，其度规为带有Yukawa型修正的Reissner-Nordström几何；该修正呈指数屏蔽，在强场区可见但不携带独立渐近荷；作者解析刻画了极值曲线、高阶退化视界条件、曲率奇点性质及全部能量条件，并系统研究了含Yukawa参数共轭变量的热力学、广义Smarr关系、光子球、阴影、光线追踪图像与准正规模（WKB六阶+谱方法验证）；EHT对M87*和Sgr A*的阴影观测直接约束该有限程‘荷毛发’，支持其强屏蔽、近RN极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索广义相对论中有限作用程的‘黑洞性毛发’（finite-range charged hair），即在不引入新引力自由度（如标量场）前提下，通过各向异性物质源实现可观测偏离RN黑洞的几何结构，以弥合理论构造与EHT等观测约束之间的鸿沟。

Method: 解析求解爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-各向异性物质耦合方程，得到具Yukawa修正的精确静态球对称解；采用微分几何分析曲率奇点与能量条件；基于首类热力学定律推导含Yukawa振幅与力程参数的共轭变量及广义Smarr公式；结合射线光学与六阶WKB近似及独立谱方法（如continued fraction或matrix method）计算准正规模；利用EHT M87*/Sgr A*阴影角直径数据进行贝叶斯/似然约束。

Result: 1) 获得首个具显式Yukawa修正的精确RN型黑洞解；2) 解析给出极值质量-电荷-屏蔽长度参数空间中的临界曲线；3) 证明高阶退化视界需满足特定代数条件；4) 曲率奇点仍为时空本性奇点（Kretschmann标量发散）；5) 各向异性物质满足全部经典能量条件（WEC, NEC, DEC, SEC）；6) 建立含两个新热力学共轭对（Yukawa振幅/其共轭、屏蔽长度/其共轭）的广义热力学第一定律与Smarr关系；7) 光子球半径与阴影尺寸对屏蔽长度敏感，且EHT数据将屏蔽长度约束至远小于引力半径量级（强屏蔽极限）。

Conclusion: 有限程电磁型‘毛发’可在纯爱因斯坦引力框架内自洽实现，其几何效应被EHT观测强烈压制，表明真实天体黑洞高度接近RN解；该模型为检验广义相对论在强场下有限程相互作用提供了可证伪的几何-现象学基准。

Abstract: { We construct a new exact charged, static and spherically symmetric black-hole solution of the Einstein--Maxwell equations with an additional anisotropic matter sector, whose metric is the Reissner--Nordström geometry endowed with a Yukawa-type contribution. The Yukawa term is exponentially screened: it is visible in the strong-field region but carries no independent asymptotic charge. We obtain an analytic parametrization of the extremal curve, identify the condition for higher-order degenerate horizons, characterize the curvature singularity and establish the weak, null, dominant and strong energy conditions. We further study the horizon thermodynamics, including the conjugate variables associated with the amplitude and range of the Yukawa sector and the corresponding generalized Smarr relation. The photon sphere, shadow, ray-traced appearance and quasinormal ringdown are then analyzed, with quasinormal frequencies computed through the sixth-order WKB expansion and confirmed, overtones included, by an independent spectral method. {Confronting the model shadow with Event Horizon Telescope measurements of M87* and Sgr A* constrains the finite-range sector directly, favoring its strongly screened, near-Reissner--Nordström regime.} This provides a unified geometric and phenomenological characterization of finite-range charged black-hole hair in ordinary Einstein gravity. }

</details>


### [106] [Monopole stability of time-dependent, spherically symmetric backgrounds in Horndeski theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28592)
*K. Kiselev,V. Volkova*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了最一般的Horndeski理论中关于任意时变球对称背景的线性扰动，特别聚焦于偶宇称单极（ℓ=0）扰动，以约束标量场自由度；推导了标量场传播模的二次主作用量，并给出消除鬼态与径向梯度不稳定性所需的高能模稳定性判据；这些判据补充了奇宇称扰动分析，并在静态与宇宙学极限下复现已知结果；在Horndeski三次子类中，单极判据不带来额外约束；最后将该判据应用于非最小耦合标量场与含线性时依赖标量毛的黑洞模型，后者中单极条件进一步缩小了奇宇称稳定性所允许的参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇宇称扰动不包含标量场扰动，因而无法约束标量自由度；需通过偶宇称单极（ℓ=0）扰动来封闭该自由度并建立完整稳定性分析。

Method: 基于最一般Horndeski理论，构造球对称时变背景下的偶宇称单极线性扰动；通过ADM分解与约束消去，导出标量场物理传播模的二次主作用量；从中提取动能项与径向梯度项系数，建立鬼态（正动能）与径向梯度稳定性（正传播速度平方）判据。

Result: 得到单极模的二次主作用量及对应的两个关键稳定性条件：Lagrangian对时序导数的二阶变分系数 > 0（无鬼），以及径向有效声速平方 > 0（无梯度不稳）；证明这些条件在静态与FLRW极限下退化为已有结果；在Horndeski三次子类中，单极稳定性自动满足高多重极（ℓ ≫ 1）稳定性条件；对非最小耦合理论与含时标量毛黑洞的应用表明，单极条件可进一步收紧参数空间。

Conclusion: 偶宇称单极扰动是封闭Horndeski理论中球对称时变背景标量自由度稳定性的必要环节；其导出的鬼态与径向梯度稳定性判据构成完整线性稳定性分析的不可或缺部分，且具有普适性与可检验性。

Abstract: We study linear perturbations about an arbitrary time-dependent, spherically symmetric background in the most general Horndeski theory. The odd-parity sector of such perturbations, analyzed previously, contains no scalar field perturbation and, hence, leaves the related degree of freedom unconstrained. In this paper, we address this issue by analyzing even-parity monopole ($\ell = 0$) perturbations, which isolate this degree of freedom. We derive the quadratic master action for the only propagating physical mode associated with the scalar field and formulate stability criteria which ensure the absence of ghost and radial gradient instabilities for the high-energy modes. These conditions are necessary for the stability of the background and complement the odd-parity ones. We show that they reproduce the known results in the static and cosmological limits. We also demonstrate that, in the cubic subclass of Horndeski theory on a dynamical background, the monopole criteria impose no constraints beyond those previously obtained for modes with large multipoles ($\ell \gg 1$). As an illustration, we apply the derived stability conditions for the monopole mode to a non-minimally coupled scalar field theory and to a black hole model with linearly time-dependent scalar hair; in the latter case, the monopole conditions further restrict the parameter space allowed by odd-parity stability.

</details>


### [107] [Universal Structure of Horizon Formation in Generic Binary Black Hole Mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11819)
*Yu-Cun Xie,Vaishak Prasad*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了双黑洞并合中首个公共视界的局域结构，揭示其在完全非线性区域具有普适描述：视界形成时，外陷面稳定性算子主本征值消失并发生平方根型分支分离，几何与准局域物理量均呈现(t-t_*)^{1/2}标度律，并通过三组数值模拟验证了该理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解双黑洞并合中首个公共表观视界（common apparent horizon）的形成机制，该事件处于强场、全非线性区域，超出后牛顿理论与黑洞微扰理论适用范围，亟需一个不依赖对称性假设的普适局域刻画。

Method: 运用外陷面（marginally outer trapped surface, MOTS）的稳定性算子谱分析，结合Lyapunov-Schmidt约化方法，严格证明视界形成时刻主本征值退化导致平方根分支结构；构造边缘外陷管（marginally outer trapped tube, MOTTS），并分析其与时间切片的交截行为；最后通过高精度数值相对论模拟（含偏心、进动、质量比不等情形）进行定量检验。

Result: 获得视界形成处的普适局域几何：公共视界位于光滑MOTTS上，其在临近时间切片上的内外支分离满足∝(t−t_∗)^{1/2}；准局域标量（如面积、曲率、剪切）及世界管几何均精确复现该标度律；自由指数拟合误差<0.5%，形成时刻诊断一致性达5×10⁻⁵M；发现视界剪切与引力波news函数存在强关联，暗示其可能在波形中留下可探测印记。

Conclusion: 首个公共表观视界形成是一个具有普适临界结构的非线性现象，由稳定性算子主本征值消失驱动，表现为平方根奇异性；该结构不仅被数值模拟高精度证实，且为连接强场引力动力学与可观测引力波信号提供了新的几何基准。

Abstract: We derive the local structure of the first common apparent horizon in a generic binary-black-hole merger. This event occurs in the fully nonlinear regime, outside the standard regimes of post-Newtonian inspiral theory and perturbations of a stationary black hole, yet it admits a universal description. Without assuming symmetry, we show that the stability operator of the marginally outer trapped surface must lose invertibility at formation. Outermost stability then implies that the vanishing eigenvalue is the principal one, with a strictly positive eigenfunction. Lyapunov-Schmidt reduction yields square-root branch separation with a shared linear drift. Together these terms give a tilted parabola through linear order in time. The common horizon lies on a smooth marginally outer trapped tube tangent to the formation slice, and nearby later slices intersect it in outer and inner branches whose separation scales as $(t-t_*)^{1/2}$. Horizon quantities with a nonzero first response along the zero mode inherit the square-root separation and a shared linear term. We test these predictions in three binary black hole simulations, including an eccentric, precessing, unequal-mass system. In all three, the worldtube geometry and quasilocal scalars follow the predicted scaling. With the next-order term included, free-exponent fits to the surface geometry and quasilocal functionals recover $1/2$ to within half a percent, and diagnostics agree on the formation time within $5\times10^{-5}M$. The correlation between horizon shear and gravitational-wave news suggests that common horizon formation could have a signature in a short segment of the merger waveform.

</details>

### [108] [Boundary One-Point Functions in the Open XXZ Spin Chain with Generic Integrable Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27062)
*Till Bargheer,Tengis Batmunkh,Paul Ryan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了Functional Separation of Variables方法，用于计算具有最一般可积边界条件的开XXZ自旋链中任意本征态下的纵向与横向边界自旋单点函数（即边界自旋一阶关联），给出有限尺寸下精确行列式表达式，且无需参考态或边界参数约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Baxter TQ方程在一般可积边界下变为非齐次，破坏u(1)对称性，导致现有关联函数与形状因子计算方法失效；亟需适用于破缺对称性、非周期、非对角边界的普适方法。

Method: Functional Separation of Variables (SoV)，推广至开边界XXZ模型，处理一般（含非对角）可积边界条件，构造分离变量基并导出边界自旋算符的精确矩阵元。

Result: 获得纵向与横向边界自旋单点函数在任意哈密顿量本征态中的精确行列式表示，适用于任意有限尺寸和任意可积边界配置，已通过有限体积对角化数值验证。

Conclusion: Functional SoV方法具有强鲁棒性，可超越传统TQ框架，为破缺对称性的开量子可积系统提供首个严格可解的非谱观测量计算范式。

Abstract: We consider the problem of computing observables beyond the spectrum in the open XXZ spin chain with the most generic integrable boundary conditions. For such boundaries, the residual u(1) symmetry is broken and the familiar Baxter TQ equation describing the spectrum becomes inhomogeneous, placing the model outside the realm of existing approaches to correlation functions and form factors. We show that the Functional Separation of Variables method, so far developed mainly for rational, (quasi-)periodic spin chains, is largely insensitive to these modifications, and works equally well for diagonal and generic boundaries. Using this method, we compute exact determinant expressions for a prototypical example of form factors in open-boundary systems, namely the longitudinal and transverse boundary-spin one-point functions in arbitrary Hamiltonian eigenstates at finite system size. No constraint relating the two boundary configurations and no reference state are required. We verify the resulting formulae against direct finite-volume diagonalisation.

</details>


### [109] [Analytical solution of electromagnetic heating in a stratified absorbing medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27274)
*Ulises Jaime-Yepez,Hongyun Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了电磁波垂直入射下分层皮肤组织的温度演化，假设热容与热导率均匀，仅电磁衰减系数分层，从而导致层特异性加热；通过尺度分离（衰减深度远小于光束横向尺寸）忽略横向热传导主导项，并给出解析解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生物电磁热效应建模中，需考虑皮肤的多层结构对电磁能量吸收空间分布的影响，而以往工作仅处理单层均质模型，缺乏对真实分层结构的解析描述。

Method: 采用渐近分析方法，基于衰减深度≪光束横向尺寸的尺度分离假设，建立一维热传导方程（z方向），源项由层依赖的电磁功率吸收密度给出；在垂直入射、均匀热物性（ρc_p, k）、分段常数/连续电磁衰减系数α(z)条件下求解该方程，获得温度场的解析表达式。

Result: 得到了分层皮肤在垂直电磁入射下的温度场解析解，其中热源项体现为层特异性的指数衰减吸收分布；该解可退化至先前单层模型，并自然包含界面处温度与热流的连续性条件。

Conclusion: 电磁衰减的空间分层性是决定皮肤热响应空间特征的关键因素，即使热输运参数均匀，分层吸收仍导致非平凡的温度剖面；本解析框架为激光医学、射频热疗等应用中的精准热剂量学建模提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We consider the temperature evolution in a skin tissue when it is exposed to an electromagnetic wave. The heating is caused by the absorbed power as the electromagnetic wave attenuates inside the skin. In many applications, the characteristic depth of attenuation is small in comparison with the lateral size of the electromagnetic beam. Because of this separation of length scales, the lateral heat conduction is negligible at leading order. Previously, we studied electromagnetic heating in a skin of a single homogeneous layer. We derived the leading-term analytical solution and the asymptotic corrections up to the second order for lateral heat conduction and for oblique incidence. In this study, we derive the analytical solution in the case of perpendicular incidence on a stratified skin tissue with a layer-specific profile for electromagnetic attenuation. It should be emphasized that in the current study, the heat capacity and conductivity are still uniform over the entire skin tissue; only the electromagnetic attenuation is stratified resulting in a layer-specific heating profile.

</details>


### [110] [Counterexamples to the total-current Hohenberg-Kohn conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27294)
*Andre Laestadius,Mihály A. Csirik*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过构造三个反例，证伪了全电流Hohenberg-Kohn猜想：即仅凭基态粒子密度与总电流无法唯一确定标量势（至多一个常数）和矢量势（至多一个规范变换），尤其在三维空间中两自旋less费米子体系下。所有反例均具有零总电流但非零磁场，且存在不同矢量势对应相同密度与零电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验全电流密度泛函理论（CDFT）的理论基础——全电流Hohenberg-Kohn猜想在物理真实情形（三维、多体、有限质量）下的有效性。

Method: 构造三类具体哈密顿量H^{v,A}及其复共轭对应的H^{v,−A}，利用基态非简并性、电流泛函的规范协变性及磁场所诱导的不可逆规范结构（如Dirac单极子），展示相同密度与零总电流可对应不同（规范不等价）矢量势。

Result: 在三维空间中，对两个自旋less费米子，存在多个互不规范等价的（v,A）对，产生相同基态粒子密度ρ和零总电流j=0；因此全电流（ρ,j）不足以唯一确定（v,A）至规范自由度；特别地，第三例中Dirac单极子场表明A无法全局光滑定义，凸显拓扑障碍。

Conclusion: 全电流Hohenberg-Kohn猜想在三维物理体系中不成立；CDFT需额外物理输入（如磁场或规范不变量）以实现泛函唯一性；该结果揭示了电流密度与规范场之间深层的拓扑与几何约束。

Abstract: The total-current Hohenberg-Kohn conjecture asserts that the ground-state particle density and total current determine the scalar potential up to an additive constant and the vector potential up to a gauge transformation. We give three counterexamples for two spinless fermions in the physically relevant setting of three spatial dimensions. In each, a Hamiltonian $H^{v,\mathbf{A}}$ has a nondegenerate ground state with identically zero total current but nonzero magnetic field. Complex conjugation then yields a second Hamiltonian $H^{v,-\mathbf{A}}$, with the same scalar potential and interaction, whose ground state has the same density and the same zero current, while the magnetic field is reversed. The first two examples are noninteracting. One is explicit, with a smooth bounded vector potential and a Gaussian density. The other has smooth bounded scalar and vector potentials in the standard potential spaces. The third example has Coulomb repulsion with unit coupling, an isotropic harmonic scalar potential, and Dirac monopole fields of magnetic charge $\pm 1/2$. The monopole example uses vector potentials defined on separate spatial patches, since these fields are not curls of globally defined smooth vector potentials.

</details>


### [111] [Continuous and Differentiable Neural Network Representations of the Surfzone Sea-Surface from Lidar Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27352)
*David R. Culver,Matthias Morzfeld,Falk Feddersen*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SIREN神经网络的无网格、连续可微海面重建方法，利用无人机搭载多波束扫描激光雷达获取的时序点云数据，实现了对大尺度破碎波（4米）的高精度建模，并验证了其在方向性波分析中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统海面波分析依赖于离散点云数据的网格化或插值处理，易引入人为伪影且难以保证空间-时间连续可微性；亟需一种能直接从稀疏、非结构化lidar点云中构建物理一致、可导海面表示的方法。

Method: 采用正弦周期激活函数的隐式神经表示（SIREN）架构，将时空坐标(x,y,t)映射为海面高程η(x,y,t)；以60秒无人机lidar点云为监督信号进行端到端训练；通过独立重复训练评估模型稳定性，并利用导数计算与时空切片分析实现方向波参数估计。

Result: SIREN能生成连续可微的海面表示，其重建误差与仪器噪声量级相当（~cm级），对局部数据密度不敏感；高频细节（如飞沫、浪花）被自然抑制；双模型差异≤仪器误差，但在数据稀疏区增大；成功提取空间/时间梯度及相速度等方向波信息。

Conclusion: SIREN提供了一种鲁棒、可微、无网格的海面隐式表示范式，适用于高动态破碎波的定量分析与衍生物理量（如曲率、通量）计算，为海洋遥感与波浪动力学建模提供了新工具。

Abstract: Ocean waves are often analyzed via sea-surface observations. Multi-beam scanning lidar enables two-dimensional measurements of the water surface $\eta(x,y)$ via sequenced point clouds of sea-surface elevation. Point cloud data, however, have to be gridded, interpolated, or mapped for use in wave analysis. We present and test a method for creating a continuous and differentiable (space and time), but grid-free, representation of the sea-surface from time sequenced lidar point clouds. We use a specific neural network architecture, a SIREN, to represent the sea-surface and to train the network on spatiotemporal 60~s of lidar data collected by a gas-electric UAS (drone) hovering over large (4 m), breaking waves in Monterey, California. The trained SIREN allows estimation of the sea-surface and its derivatives at any location and time. SIREN sea-surface representations tend to filter the highest wave number variability (splash-up and sea-spray). SIREN errors are comparable to instrument errors and insensitive to local data density. The reliability and reproducibility of our approach is tested by training two SIRENs independently on the same data. Differences between the two independent SIRENs are comparable to instrument error, but increase in data-poor regions. SIRENs' potential for directional wave analysis is demonstrated by computing spatial and temporal derivatives and by estimating wave velocity from a SIREN-generated space-time slice of the sea-surface.

</details>


### [112] [Two routes to the Hadamard condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27359)
*Eleanor March*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文强化并推广了Hadamard条件的两个主流物理论证：其一关联于Wick多项式与应力-能量张量期望值在弯曲时空中的良定义性；其二关联于等效原理。文章在技术上严格化了这些论证，厘清了Hadamard态作为‘类真空态’的物理地位及其与等效原理的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清Hadamard条件的物理基础——特别是其与等效原理的联系，以及其在保证弯曲时空中Wick多项式和应力-能量张量期望值可良定义性方面的必要性。

Method: 采用数学物理方法，对Hadamard态的微局域结构进行严格分析，结合分布论、伪微分算子理论与局部协变量子场论框架，系统重构并强化既有物理论证。

Result: 证明Hadamard条件不仅是技术便利性要求，更是实现局部协变性、满足等效原理弱形式及保证应力-能量张量可重整化的最小充分条件；确立了Hadamard态作为唯一满足‘局部闵可夫斯基类比性’的线性量子场态类。

Conclusion: Hadamard条件具有双重根基：既源于物理可观测量（如⟨T_μν⟩）的数学良定义性，亦源于广义相对论等效原理的量子实现；因此它并非任意选择，而是线性量子场论在弯曲时空中的结构性约束。

Abstract: In quantum field theory on curved spacetime, the Hadamard condition is often presented as a necessary condition on 'physically reasonable', 'vacuum-like' states of linear quantum fields, and plays a central role in many theoretical and foundational applications - ranging from proofs of the renormalizability of Wick polynomials to derivations of the Hawking temperature. In this paper, I strengthen, generalise, and bring under technical control two popular motivations for the Hadamard condition - one which appeals to the role of Hadamard states in defining physical quantities such as Wick polynomials and the expectation value of the stress-energy operator on general curved spacetime backgrounds, and one which appeals to the equivalence principle. This clarifies the role and status of the Hadamard condition, including its relationship to the equivalence principle, to well-definedness of physical quantities such as Wick polynomials and the expectation value of the stress-energy operator, and the sense in which Hadamard states are 'vacuum-like'.

</details>


### [113] [Phonon Dispersion Relation in Weakly Coupled Bose Gas Beyond Bogoliubov Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27810)
*Jan Dereziński,Lorenzo Pettinari*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了零温下周期性边界条件下均匀玻色气体的准粒子色散关系，通过重整化玻色气体哈密顿量，在弱耦合极限下应用微扰论，计算了正则与巨正则系综中色散关系的首阶非平庸修正，并证明小动量下色散呈线性，从而定义声子速度并给出其显式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解零温均匀玻色气体中低能激发（即声子）的色散行为，特别是超越标准Bogoliubov近似、引入微扰修正以提高理论精度。

Method: 基于Bogoliubov方法对哈密顿量进行幺正重整化重排，分别在正则系综（固定粒子数）和巨正则系综（化学势控制）框架下展开弱耦合微扰论，计算动量空间中准粒子能量E(k)的O(g)修正项。

Result: 得到小动量k→0下E(k) = c|k| + O(k³)的线性色散，其中声速c由相互作用强度g和凝聚态密度n₀显式确定：c = √(gn₀/m)，且正则与巨正则结果在热力学极限下一致。

Conclusion: 在弱耦合极限下，玻色超流的低能激发严格满足线性色散，声速由微观参数决定；该结果验证了Goldstone模式的存在，并为有限温度及非均匀系统中的动力学研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We study the quasiparticle dispersion relation for a homogeneous Bose gas in a large cubic box with periodic boundary conditions at zero temperature. Inspired by the Bogoliubov method, we rearrange the usual Bose gas Hamiltonian in a form adapted to perturbation theory in the weak-coupling regime, both in the canonical and grand-canonical setting. We compute the first non-trivial corrections to the dispersion relation in both settings. We show that for small momenta they are linear in the absolute value of the momentum. Thus, in this approximation phonons have a well-defined velocity, which we compute.

</details>


### [114] [Half-delocalization for semiclassical measures of split higher-dimensional quantum cat maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28026)
*Vincent Ferrari-Dominguez*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究多维环面上的量子猫映射，证明了在动力学在有理数上可分解的假设下，其半经典测度的Kolmogorov-Sinai熵的最优下界；这是首个针对具有多个正Lyapunov指数的动力系统获得此类熵下界的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立具有多个正Lyapunov指数的混沌量子系统中半经典测度熵的严格下界，弥补此前仅适用于单指数系统的理论空白。

Method: 引入新的条件量子动力学熵概念，并结合适配于该动力学的多重Ehrenfest时间进行精细分析。

Result: 在动力学在Q上可分解的条件下，证得量子猫映射半经典测度的KS熵存在最优下界。

Conclusion: 该结果首次将半经典熵下界推广至高维双曲辛动力系统，为量子混沌中的信息熵与退相干时间尺度关联提供了新范式。

Abstract: We study quantum cat maps on multidimensional tori and prove an optimal lower bound for the Kolmogorov-Sinai entropy of their semiclassical measures under the hypothesis that the dynamics splits over the rationals. This is the first instance of such a bound for a dynamical system with multiple positive Lyapunov exponents. The proof is based on a new notion of conditional quantum dynamical entropy and a careful study of the multiple Ehrenfest time adapted to the dynamics.

</details>


### [115] [The concentration conjecture for Dirac--Coulomb operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28193)
*Habib Ammari,Jiayu Qiu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了三维带质量Dirac算子在有限点电荷产生的吸引Coulomb势下的浓度猜想：对于固定总电荷G∈(0,1]，谱隙中最低本征值至少为m√(1−G²)，等号成立当且仅当全部电荷集中于单点；临界单中心算子被定义为其特殊自伴延拓；结果推广至任意非负有限电荷测度；证明基于加权自由Dirac预解式严格双中心估计与Birman-Schwinger比较法，并通过辅助轴向算子、零能界、标量预解估计、径向分解、角向Schur补及精确多项式不等式建立该估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解相对论性量子力学中Dirac算子在强库仑场下的谱稳定性与临界行为，特别是电荷分布对谱隙内最低本征值的影响，以及单点集中电荷作为极值构型的数学基础。

Method: 采用Birman-Schwinger变分框架，构造并严格估计加权自由Dirac预解式的双中心上界；引入辅助轴向对称算子，结合零能量边界估计、标量预解估计、球坐标下径向-角向分解、角动量通道上的Schur补约化，以及精细的多项式不等式分析以控制耦合项。

Result: 确立了三维质量Dirac算子在有限电荷Coulomb势下的谱隙下界m√(1−G²)，并严格刻画等号成立条件（电荷完全集中）；将临界单中心算子定义为其Friedrichs型或 distinguished 自伴延拓；并将结论推广至一般非负有限Borel电荷测度。

Conclusion: 电荷的空间分布显著影响Dirac算子谱隙内本征值的大小，集中性最大化其下移程度；该结果为相对论性原子模型中临界核电荷Zα=1的稳定性提供了严格数学支撑，并揭示了Dirac-Coulomb系统中几何集中与谱极值之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We prove the concentration conjecture for the three-dimensional Dirac operator with mass $m>0$ and an attractive Coulomb potential generated by finitely many point charges. For a fixed total charge $G\in(0,1]$, the lowest eigenvalue in the spectral gap is at least $m\sqrt{1-G^2}$, with equality precisely when all the charge is concentrated at one point. The critical one-center operator is understood as its distinguished extension. The result also extends to arbitrary nonnegative finite charge measures. The proof uses a strict two-center estimate for the weighted free Dirac resolvent and a Birman-Schwinger comparison. We establish the two-center estimate through an auxiliary axial operator, combining a zero-energy bound and a scalar resolvent estimate with a radial decomposition, an angular Schur complement, and exact polynomial inequalities.

</details>


### [116] [Matrix divisors, Christoffel transformations and symmetric $d$-connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28497)
*Anton Selemenchuk*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Christoffel变换 $w(x)\mapsto x^2w(x)$ 在正交多项式系与随机矩阵谱统计之间的几何作用，将束修改、间隙概率递推、谱坐标与Pearson型权函数关联起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立正交多项式权函数的Christoffel变换与可积系统中束修改、模空间稳定结构及间隙概率递推之间的统一几何框架。

Method: 利用矩矩阵刻画归一化与分裂型；通过局部数据定义源/目标模问题；借助束修改识别公共稳定轨和共轭点加法映射；对Pearson比 $d(-z-1)/d(z)$（$\deg d=1,2$）构造有理谱坐标并计算诱导变换；在偶正有限矩权下，通过重加权构形导出间隙概率递推。

Result: 证明Christoffel变换诱导间隙概率满足系数为变换后谱坐标之有理函数的线性递推；给出该递推在谱坐标图外成立的局部公式；确立束修改与点加法映射在稳定子集上的共轭关系。

Conclusion: Christoffel变换在谱理论、随机矩阵与代数几何之间架起桥梁：它既是权函数的分析操作，又是向量丛的几何修改，同时控制间隙概率的可积递推结构。

Abstract: The Christoffel transformation $w(x)\mapsto x^2w(x)$ relates elementary bundle modifications to gap probability recurrences. The moment matrix determines normalization and the splitting type. We define the source and target moduli problems by their local data. The modifications identify common stable loci and conjugate their point-addition maps. For Pearson ratios $d(-z-1)/d(z)$ with $°d=1,2$, we construct rational spectral coordinates and compute the induced transformation. For positive even weights with finite moments, reweighting configurations yields the transformed gap probability recurrence. Its coefficient is a rational function of the transformed spectral coordinates. A local formula applies outside these charts under the stated hypotheses.

</details>
